Management Message   “Boost 2028”   Value Creation Process   Business Overview      Governance              Sustainability     Data




Publication of the 2026 Integrated Report


                                                        Guided by our mission to broadly contribute to society through value creation based on innovative research and
                                                        development activities for the betterment of healthcare and fuller lives of people worldwide, the Sumitomo Pharma
                                                        Group aims to contribute to the realization of a sustainable society and the sustained enhancement of corporate
                                                        value. We regard this integrated report as a key communication tool for helping shareholders, investors, and other
                                                        stakeholders understand our medium- to long-term management policies and initiatives based on that Mission, and
                                                        for fostering constructive dialogue with them.
                                                             The Sumitomo Pharma Group faced a challenging business environment in FY2023. Since then, through
                                                        fundamental structural reforms, we have worked to rebuild our earnings structure and financial foundation. In FY2025,
                                                        our business performance recovered significantly, enabling us to achieve the financial targets set out in Reboot 2027
                                                        ahead of schedule. I believe this achievement reflects the collective efforts of the entire Group to reshape our business
                                                        portfolio, reform our cost structure, and reorganize our R&D structure.
                                                             However, our efforts to enhance corporate value do not end once with the completion of our restructuring. What
                                                        matters is ensuring that this recovery is not temporary, but that we continue to drive the Value Creation Cycle as
                                                        an R&D-driven pharmaceutical company. To that end, we have launched Boost 2028 as our growth strategy through
                                                        FY2028. By maximizing the earnings generated by our core products and reinvesting those funds in oncology
                                                        development candidates, regenerative medicine/cell therapy, and next-generation pipeline assets, we will accelerate
                                                        the transition from value creation to value delivery.
                                                             This report provides a detailed account of our transition from restructuring to renewed growth through messages
                                                        from the management team and information on our business strategies, research and development, human
                                                        resources, and corporate governance. In particular, as a result of our research and development, the conditional
                                                        and time-limited approval of AMCHEPRY®—the world’s first iPS cell-derived pharmaceutical product—in Japan is a
                                                        symbolic accomplishment that has truly delivered new value to patients and brought renewed energy to the Company.
                                                        Going forward, we will steadily translate the value generated through research and development into business growth
                                                        and demonstrate through tangible results that we are making steady progress toward renewed growth.
                                                             We recognize that regaining the confidence of the capital markets remains an important challenge. We must
                                                        confront these challenges sincerely and deliver results across earnings, R&D, capital policy, and shareholder returns—
                                                        not merely words. Through this report, I hope you will see that the Company has moved beyond the restructuring
                                                        phase and taken a solid step toward sustainable growth. We will continue to engage in dialogue and reflect the
                                                        feedback we receive in improvements to our management and disclosure practices.


                                                                                                                                            Motoyuki Sakai
                                                                                                                                            Representative Director, Executive Vice President

“for the betterment of healthcare and fuller lives of people worldwide.”

From “Reboot” to “Boost”
Rebuilding the Value Creation Cycle
Positioning of “Boost 2028”                                                                                                                  Sumitomo Pharma Group’s vision: become a GSP
Having made progress toward achieving the financial targets of our three-year action                                                         Sumitomo Pharma’s vision for FY2033 is to become a Global Specialized Player
plan Reboot 2027 ahead of schedule, we have formulated a new growth strategy:                                                                (GSP). GSP describes the kind of company Sumitomo Pharma aspires to be: one
Boost 2028—Accelerating Strong Sumitomo Pharma—, which looks ahead to FY2028.                                                                that concentrates its resources in areas where we can leverage our strengths,
We will pursue Boost 2028 as a bridge from the achievements of structural reforms to                                                         establishes a competitive advantage globally, and achieves sustainable value creation.
rebuild the Value Creation Cycle (the “Reboot”) to the full-scale implementation of our                                                      An important step toward becoming a GSP is Boost 2028, which is a plan to enhance
growth strategy (the “Boost”), laying the groundwork for our next phase of growth.                                                           the profitability of our core products and invest in next-generation growth platforms
    Specifically, we will strengthen our revenue base by maximizing the value of our                                                         in areas where we have strengths, including the oncology, the CNS (psychiatric and
key products, particularly ORGOVYX® and GEMTESA®. At the same time, we will                                                                  neurological), and the regenerative medicine/cell therapy business.

Sumitomo Pharma’s Value Creation Process
The Sumitomo Pharma Group is working towards the realization of a sustainable society guided by our corporate Philosophy: “To broadly contribute
to society through value creation based on innovative research and development activities for the betterment of healthcare and fuller lives of people
worldwide.” To put our Philosophy into practice, we are addressing ten Material Issues such as “Development of innovative products and healthcare
solutions” through business activities that leverage our three strengths (R&D, human resources, and global platform) based on our six types of
capital, as well as providing solutions that meet diverse medical needs. We will continue to work to embody our Philosophy, aiming to establish
Sumitomo Pharma as a “Global Specialized Player (GSP).”

Value Chain Initiatives
The Sumitomo Pharma Group contributes not only to patient care but also to improving the quality of life (QOL) for patients and
their families by continually creating diverse solutions, primarily innovative pharmaceutical products through its value chain.

*1	Circles ( ) for Internal Directors indicate knowledge, experience and skills cultivated through the relevant person’s career, etc. Circles for Outside Directors indicate knowledge, experience and skills expected of them based on the relevant person’s expertise, career, etc. The number of circles
    indicated for each Director is limited so that it does not exceed four, and therefore the matrix does not necessarily show all the knowledge, experience and skills held by the relevant person.
*2	The Board of Directors considers that it is necessary that the Directors have comprehensive knowledge, experience and skills concerning various matters, including governance, sustainability, business strategy, and global business operations as the extensive knowledge, experience and skills of a
    person who is in charge of corporate management or organizational operations in Japan or abroad.
*3 The Board of Directors considers that it is necessary that the Directors have extensive knowledge, experience and skills in different industries to offer a perspective which is different from that of the healthcare industry.
*4 The Board of Directors considers that it is necessary that the Directors have extensive knowledge, experience and skills concerning the creation and cultivation of new business or business development to contribute to the development of new business.
*5 The Board of Directors considers that it is necessary that the Directors have extensive knowledge, experience and skills concerning digital technologies and data utilization to contribute to the creation of new value through digital technology or data utilization.
